HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001","root_000661/B002","root_000661/B003","root_001525/B001","root_001525/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_0dc7fedb78fd6b7b3583","text":"2. **The asker/requester is reversed.** Branch evidence: س ء ل B001, asking/requesting; س ء ل B002, the sought request; س ء ل B003, fulfilling a request; ن ع م B001, benefaction given to another; ن ع م B004, answering yes/affirming. Structural cue: the passive \"لتسألن\" hides the interrogator while foregrounding the addressees as questioned. Assigned role: earlier نعيم implies received gifts and fulfilled wants; سأل reverses the direction of asking. Abductive move: I infer that the ones whose requests were met now become the respondents. Reading change: the ayah reads as a reversal from \"having one's wants answered\" to \"having to answer for what was wanted and granted.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B011","root_001525/B012"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_12c6a7bed3649e326404","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B011\",\"B012\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sorgu, nimetin insanı yerleştirdiği mekânları ve rahatlık ya da çaba içinde yön verdiği yaşam yolunu da kapsar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2290b0b6d453661cae21","text":"No later ayah lies inside this fixed ayah's clipped 102:3–8 focus window, so no later retrospective surprise emerged.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_278c78a1d22f85ce4866","text":"4. **The gladdened eye brought into the account.** Fixed `ن ع م B013` contains the accepted formulaic image of an eye gladdened or honored by favor, activated by adjacent `عَيْنَ ٱلْيَقِينِ` (`ع ي ن B001/B002`) in 102:7. The sequence couples the seeing faculty with the benefit under question. Eye-gladdening is assigned the role of a concrete instance of enjoyed favor, while direct sight supplies its reversal into painful certainty. I infer that pleasures delivered through perception are included in the questioned `ٱلنَّعِيمِ`; the fixed noun does not enumerate them. The final noun therefore gathers the preceding eye into its scope: the same capacity that enjoyed pleasing sights now witnesses and answers.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002","root_001525/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_28ebade08fbea0bbfc15","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B002\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sorgunun konusu yalnız rahatlık duygusu değil, bu rahatlığı taşıyan hayvanlar ve somut geçim varlıklarıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001","root_001525/B001","root_001525/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a45838b52d122da40fc","text":"1. **Favor converted into interrogation.** Branch evidence: س ء ل B001, asking/questioning; ن ع م B001, pleasant well-being and bestowed favor; ن ع م B002, softness/easeful living; ل ه و B001, diversion; ك ث ر B002, rivalry. Structural cue: the final \"ثم\" follows knowledge and sight, and the preposition \"عن\" directs the question toward النعيم. Assigned role: نعيم is the enjoyed field; سأل turns it into the object of account. Abductive move: I infer that ease becomes answerable precisely because it had functioned as distraction and rivalry-material. Reading change: النعيم reads not as simple comfort but as favor placed under examination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2b51cf1b257ebf0bf02d","text":"3. **Soft ease exposed beside severe heat.** Fixed `ن ع م B002` contributes softness, comfort, pampering, and easeful living; contextual `ج ح م B001` in 102:6 supplies blazing fire and severe heat. The sequence places the sight of the blaze immediately before direct certainty and then the question about ease. The two material branches serve as opposed bodily environments: insulation and softness versus exposure and burning intensity. I infer that the later environment reveals the moral non-neutrality of the earlier one; the text does not state that comfort causes the blaze. On this activation, `ٱلنَّعِيمِ` becomes sharply tactile: it is precisely the ease whose taken-for-granted shelter is broken by what has just been seen.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B013"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-8","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2b7497ce30d5e5a5cf55","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B013\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gözü sevindiren duyusal hazlar da sorgulanan nimetin somut bir parçası olur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001","root_001525/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_549150db1d56623ee227","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:2\",\"root_000661\",[\"B001\"]],[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sorgu, nimetlerin çokluk yarışında dikkat dağıtıcı ve üstünlük sağlayan malzeme yapılmasının hesabını açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001286/B003","root_001525/B002","root_001525/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_596bca3115feca22ef9f","text":"3. **Softness and livestock become concrete assets.** Branch evidence: ن ع م B002, soft/easeful living; ن ع م B005, grazing livestock especially camels; ع ي ن B011, present cash; ك ث ر B003, wealth/claims attached to persons. Structural cue: the final object النعيم is singularly broad after earlier competitive increase and eye-certainty. Assigned role: نعيم expands from pleasant feeling to bodily softness, animals, wealth, and tangible assets. Abductive move: I infer that the question reaches both comfort-experience and material supports of comfort. Reading change: the final question becomes materially concrete, not an abstract question about \"blessings\" alone.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6c430449c9571e711c8a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ateşin şiddetli sıcaklığından sonra gelen sorgu, daha önce doğal sayılan yumuşaklık ve rahatlığı bedensel olarak görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001195/B001","root_001525/B011","root_001525/B012"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_71e66c396c3f99e3f7e5","text":"4. **Settlement, walking, and the grave-visit return.** Branch evidence: ن ع م B011, a place agreeing with someone/pleasant settlement; ن ع م B012, going on foot or using the feet; ز و ر B003, visiting; ق ب ر B001, graves. Structural cue: the surah began with being driven until a visit, and ends with a day-of-questioning after another \"ثم.\" Assigned role: نعيم B011/B012 rereads life as places one settled into and paths one walked by ease or exertion. Abductive move: I infer that the question \"about نعيم\" includes how comfort shaped movement and dwelling. Reading change: the final ayah asks not only what goods were enjoyed, but what kind of journey and settlement those goods produced.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8dc630a429b2589156e1","text":"5. **Exploratory: fulfilled needs reverse into a demand for an answer.** Fixed `س ء ل B003` preserves the dormant sense of fulfilling someone's request, and fixed `ن ع م B001` supplies benefaction granted to another. In the fixed construction, however, the recipients themselves are emphatically questioned. The two branches create a reversal of direction: prior needs move toward fulfillment, then a question moves back toward the beneficiaries. I supply the assumption that at least some favors can be construed as answers to needs; the morphological link is weak because passive `تُسْـَٔلُنَّ` directly realizes `B001`, not `B003`. The ayah can nevertheless read as the return side of receiving: what arrived as provision now requires a response from the one provided for.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_97e1d361c38c9793377d","text":"2. **Favor as an entrusted transfer, not a self-produced condition.** Fixed `ن ع م B001` specifically includes benefaction conveyed to another, as well as ease and good condition. The preposition `عَنِ` makes that transferred good the topic of questioning, and the passive plural makes its recipients the questioned party. Benefaction is assigned the social role of something received and therefore traceable between giver and beneficiary. I supply the normative arrow that a transferred benefit can carry an obligation to account for its use. `ٱلنَّعِيمِ` thus shifts from private pleasure to entrusted bounty whose source and disposition matter.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b491575ccaf0291cd29e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Çokluk etrafında toplanan topluluğun hesap geldiğinde dağılıp dayanışmasını yitirdiği okuması mümkündür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B002","root_000661/B003","root_001525/B001","root_001525/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c1b24126aad39982f938","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:2\",\"root_000661\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]],[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İstekleri karşılananlar şimdi, istedikleri ve kendilerine verilenler için cevap vermek zorunda kalan tarafa dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001195/B001","root_001525/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_c6e5b8f9e921dc629eed","text":"5. **Scattering after abundance.** Branch evidence: ن ع م B008, ostrich-flight image for scattering/rout/loss of cohesion; ك ث ر B007, clustering together; ك ث ر B002, rivalry by number; ق ب ر B001, graves. Structural cue: the surah moves from gathered increase to cemetery plurality and final individual questioning. Assigned role: كثر gathers; نعم B008 scatters. Abductive move: I infer that competitive cohesion dissolves when the account arrives. Reading change: النعيم can carry a surprise image of the very comfortable collectivity breaking apart under questioning.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000661/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_dad5745e03a36a7c2bc8","text":"1. **A forensic question rather than a request for missing information.** Fixed `لَتُسْـَٔلُنَّ` anchors `س ء ل B001` (asking about or requesting something), and fixed `ٱلنَّعِيمِ` anchors `ن ع م B001` (pleasant well-being and bestowed benefaction). Structurally, the passive emphatic question follows three knowings and two seeings, ending with direct certainty. Asking is assigned the role of audit, while favor supplies the matter audited. The packet does not name the questioner or state the purpose; I infer from the completed disclosure that the question seeks an account rather than new knowledge. The ayah consequently reads as compelled answerability for received good, not as an open inquiry into whether good existed.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001525/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f0e36b59e500f9533d97","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"102:8:5\",\"root_001525\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Nimet, kişinin kendi ürettiği özel bir durumdan çok, kaynağı ve nasıl kullanıldığı izlenebilen emanet edilmiş bir iyilik haline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fa8a18d35729dd210c18","text":"No later ayah remains after 102:8, so no later activation emerged.
